- **RUSSIA**: F RUM → BUL/EC (bounced); A WAR holds; A MOS holds; F BOT → SWE

### Dialogue

- **AUSTRIA → RUSSIA**: Spring 1904: F RUM is still in RUM and A BUL is still Turkish — let's finally coordinate a joint squeeze. I'm pushing A SER - BUL with F GRE support this spring; if you move F RUM - BUL/EC simultaneously, Turkey loses Bulgaria regardless of F BLA's position.
